class SourceError(RuntimeError):
    def __init__(self, reason, **details):
        super().__init__(reason)
        self.details = details



def text(value, limit=None):
    if value is None:
        return None
    value = re.sub(r'<[^>]*>', ' ', unescape(str(value)))
    value = ' '.join(value.split())
    return (value[:limit] if limit else value) or None


def number(value):
    try:
        if isinstance(value, bool) or value is None: return None
        parsed = int(str(value).replace(',', ''))
        return parsed if parsed >= 0 else None
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return None


def actual_url(value, base=None):
    if not isinstance(value, str) or not value.strip(): return None
    value = urljoin(base, value.strip()) if base else value.strip()
    try:
        p = urlsplit(value)
        if p.scheme in ('http', 'https') and p.hostname and not p.username and not p.password and len(value) <= 255:
            return value
    except ValueError:
        pass
    return None


class RealDataIngestor:
    def __init__(self, *, client=None, config=None):
        load_dotenv(PROJECT_ROOT / '.env', override=False)
        self.config = config or get_paths_config()['api']
        self.client = client
        self.reports = {}
        self.matcher = None
        self.max_pages = self.config.get('ingestion', {}).get('max_pages', 50)
        self.selection_reports = {}
        self._library_blocked = None
        for name in ('httpx', 'httpx2'):
            logging.getLogger(name).setLevel(logging.WARNING)

    def _library_cache_path(self, endpoint, params):
        # Include the credential in a one-way fingerprint, never in the file content/name.
        identity = json.dumps([endpoint,params],sort_keys=True,ensure_ascii=False)
        digest = hashlib.sha256(identity.encode('utf-8')).hexdigest()
        return PROJECT_ROOT / 'data/cache/library_api' / (digest + '.json')

    def _library_cache_read(self, path):
        try:
            saved = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
            age = time.time() - float(saved['saved_at'])
            payload = saved['payload']
            body = payload.get('response',{})
            if (0 <= age < 86400 and isinstance(body,dict) and not body.get('error')
                    and (isinstance(body.get('docs'),list) or isinstance(body.get('detail'),list) or isinstance(body.get('libs'),list))):
                return payload
        except (OSError,ValueError,KeyError,TypeError,AttributeError):
            pass
        return None

    def _library_cache_write(self, path, payload, params):
        body = payload.get('response',{})
        if (not isinstance(body,dict) or body.get('error')
                or not (isinstance(body.get('docs'),list) or isinstance(body.get('detail'),list) or isinstance(body.get('libs'),list))):
            return
        secrets = [str(v) for k,v in params.items() if 'key' in k.lower() and v]
        def redact(value):
            if isinstance(value,dict):
                return {k:redact(v) for k,v in value.items() if 'key' not in k.lower() and 'token' not in k.lower()}
            if isinstance(value,list):return [redact(v) for v in value]
            if isinstance(value,str):
                for secret in secrets:value=value.replace(secret,'[REDACTED]')
            return value
        temporary = None
        try:
            path.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
            with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(mode='w',encoding='utf-8',dir=path.parent,
                                             prefix='cache-',suffix='.tmp',delete=False) as file:
                temporary = Path(file.name)
                json.dump({'saved_at':time.time(),'payload':redact(payload)},file,ensure_ascii=False)
            os.replace(temporary,path)
        except OSError:
            self.reports['library_cache']={'status':'FAILED','reason':'CACHE_WRITE_FAILED'}
        finally:
            if temporary is not None:
                try:temporary.unlink(missing_ok=True)
                except OSError:pass

    def _request(self, url, params):
        # Only retry idempotent GET requests; never print credential-bearing exceptions.
        url = url.replace('http://', 'https://', 1)
        parsed = urlsplit(url)
        if parsed.hostname not in {'learn.microsoft.com', 'apis.data.go.kr', 'api.odcloud.kr',
                                   'data4library.kr', 'www.data4library.kr', 'www.career.go.kr'}:
            raise SourceError('UNSUPPORTED_API_HOST')
        endpoint = parsed.scheme + '://' + parsed.hostname + parsed.path
        library = parsed.hostname in {'data4library.kr','www.data4library.kr'}
        cache_path = self._library_cache_path(endpoint,params) if library else None
        if library:
            cached = self._library_cache_read(cache_path)
            if cached is not None:return cached
            if self._library_blocked is not None:
                reason,details = self._library_blocked
                raise SourceError(reason,**details)
        transient_codes = {408,429,500,502,503,504}
        for attempt in range(1,4):
            delay = 2 ** (attempt - 1)
            retryable = False
            try:
                if self.client is not None:
                    response = self.client.get(url, params=params)
                else:
                    with httpx.Client(timeout=httpx.Timeout(35,connect=10),follow_redirects=False) as client:
                        response = client.get(url, params=params)
                if response.status_code != 200:
                    reason = f'HTTP_{response.status_code}'
                    retryable = response.status_code in transient_codes
                    try:
                        retry_after = float(getattr(response,'headers',{}).get('Retry-After','0'))
                        delay = max(delay,min(30,max(0,retry_after)))
                    except (TypeError,ValueError):
                        pass
                else:
                    try:
                        payload = response.json()
                    except ValueError:
                        raise SourceError('INVALID_JSON_RESPONSE',endpoint=endpoint,attempts=attempt) from None
                    if not isinstance(payload,dict):
                        raise SourceError('INVALID_JSON_OBJECT',endpoint=endpoint,attempts=attempt)
                    if library:
                        body = payload.get('response',{})
                        error = body.get('error') if isinstance(body,dict) else None
                        if error:
                            message = str(error).lower()
                            category = 'unspecified'
                            reason = 'API_RESULT_ERROR'
                            if '500' in message and 'ip' in message and '등록' in message:
                                reason = 'LIBRARY_IP_REGISTRATION_REQUIRED'
                                category = 'daily_quota_requires_registered_ip'
                            elif any(s in message for s in ('초과','한도','소진','quota','rate limit','too many')):
                                category = 'quota_or_rate_limit'
                            elif any(s in message for s in ('인증','auth','permission','키')):
                                category = 'authentication_or_permission'
                            details = {'category':category,'endpoint':endpoint,'attempts':attempt}
                            if category != 'unspecified':
                                self._library_blocked = (reason,details)
                                self.reports['library_access']={'status':'FAILED','reason':reason,**details}
                            raise SourceError(reason,**details)
                        self._library_cache_write(cache_path,payload,params)
                    return payload
            except SourceError:
                raise
            except httpx.TransportError as exc:
                reason = type(exc).__name__
                retryable = True
            except Exception as exc:
                raise SourceError(type(exc).__name__,endpoint=endpoint,attempts=attempt) from None
            if not retryable or attempt == 3:
                raise SourceError(reason,endpoint=endpoint,attempts=attempt) from None
            logging.getLogger('LOAD_DATA').warning('%s: %s, retry %s/2 in %.0fs',
                parsed.hostname,reason,attempt,delay)
            time.sleep(delay)

    @staticmethod
    def _key(*names):
        value = next((os.getenv(n, '').strip() for n in names if os.getenv(n, '').strip()), '')
        if not value: raise SourceError('MISSING_KEY:' + '/'.join(names))
        return unquote(value)

    def _record(self, kind, source, external_key, fields, raw, match_text=None):
        if not fields.get('title') or not external_key: return None
        # Do not truncate identifiers/titles and accidentally merge unrelated entities.
        max_title = 200 if kind in ('courses', 'books') else 100
        if len(fields['title']) > max_title: return None
        for field, limit in {'provider':50, 'author':100, 'publisher':100,
                             'url':255, 'org_name':100, 'category_name':50}.items():
            if fields.get(field) is not None and len(str(fields[field])) > limit:
                fields[field] = None
        content = match_text or ' '.join(str(fields.get(k) or '') for k in
            ('title', 'syllabus_summary', 'test_subjects', 'curriculum', 'ncs_units'))
        matches = self.matcher.match(content)
        return {'kind': kind, 'source': source, 'external_key': str(external_key),
                'fields': fields, 'raw': raw, 'matches': matches,
                'fetched_at': datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()}

    def _pages(self, source, url, params, *, page_key='page', size_key='perPage', size=100, list_key='data'):
        result, signatures = [], set()
        for page in range(1, self.max_pages + 1):
            payload = self._request(url, {**params, page_key:page, size_key:size})
            if source == 'kmooc' and str(payload.get('resultCode', '00')) not in ('00', '0', '200'):
                raise SourceError('API_RESULT_ERROR')
            items = payload.get(list_key)
            if source.startswith('careernet'): items = payload.get('jobs', payload.get('dataSearch', {}).get('content'))
            if not isinstance(items, list): raise SourceError('UNEXPECTED_RESPONSE_SCHEMA')
            signature = hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(items, sort_keys=True).encode()).hexdigest()
            if items and signature in signatures: raise SourceError('PAGINATION_NOT_ADVANCING')
            signatures.add(signature)
            result.extend(items)
            self.reports[source] = {'status':'OK', 'pages_read':page, 'raw_count':len(result),
                                    'page_limit_reached':page == self.max_pages,
                                    'reported_total': number(payload.get('totalCount')),
                                    'requested_page_size':size,
                                    'reported_page_size':payload.get('header',{}).get('size',payload.get('pageSize'))}
            total = number(payload.get('totalCount'))
            if source.startswith('careernet') and items:
                total = number(payload.get('count',items[0].get('totalCount')))
                self.reports[source]['reported_total'] = total
            if not items or (total is not None and len(result) >= total):
                self.reports[source]['page_limit_reached'] = False
                break
        return result

    def fetch_courses_from_api(self, target_count=120):
        rows = []
        try:
            items = self._pages('kmooc', self.config['kmooc']['base_url'],
                {'serviceKey':self._key('KMOOC_SERVICE_KEY','DATA_GO_KR_API_KEY'),'_type':'json'},
                size_key='size', size=self.config['kmooc'].get('page_size',50), list_key='items')
            for item in items:
                key = text(item.get('id'))
                if not key or len(key) > 44: continue
                row = self._record('courses','API_KMOOC',key, {
                    'course_id':'KMOOC_'+key, 'title':text(item.get('name')),
                    'org_name':text(item.get('org_name')), 'url':actual_url(item.get('url')),
                    'category_name':None, 'is_matchup':None, 'cost':None, 'duration_weeks':None,
                    'recruitment_status':None, 'syllabus_summary':None,
                    'platform':'K-MOOC', 'difficulty':None, 'record_source':'API_KMOOC'}, item)
                if row: rows.append(row)
        except SourceError as exc:
            self.reports['kmooc'] = {'status':'FAILED','reason':str(exc)}
        try:
            ms = self.config.get('ms_learn', {})
            payload = self._request(ms.get('base_url', 'https://learn.microsoft.com/api/catalog/'), {'locale':ms.get('locale','ko-kr'),'type':'modules'})
            modules = payload.get('modules')
            if not isinstance(modules,list): raise SourceError('UNEXPECTED_RESPONSE_SCHEMA')
            self.reports['ms_learn'] = {'status':'OK','raw_count':len(modules)}
            for item in modules:
                uid = text(item.get('uid'))
                if not uid: continue
                levels = item.get('levels', [])
                difficulty = levels[0].upper() if len(levels)==1 and levels[0] in ('beginner','intermediate','advanced') else None
                row = self._record('courses','API_MS_LEARN',uid, {
                    'course_id':'MS_'+hashlib.md5(uid.encode()).hexdigest()[:12],
                    'title':text(item.get('title')), 'org_name':'Microsoft Learn',
                    'url':actual_url(item.get('url'),'https://learn.microsoft.com'),
                    'category_name':None,'is_matchup':None,
                    # Official Learn FAQ confirms module training is free; cloud usage is separate.
                    'cost':0, 'duration_weeks':None,'recruitment_status':None,
                    'syllabus_summary':text(item.get('summary'),1500), 'platform':'MS Learn',
                    'difficulty':difficulty,'record_source':'API_MS_LEARN'}, item)
                if row: rows.append(row)
        except SourceError as exc:
            self.reports['ms_learn'] = {'status':'FAILED','reason':str(exc)}
        return self._select('courses',rows,target_count)

    def _select(self, kind, rows, limit, *, include_unmatched=False):
        eligible = [r for r in rows if include_unmatched or r.get('matches')]
        selected = self.matcher.select(eligible, limit, include_unmatched=include_unmatched)
        self.selection_reports[kind] = {'normalized_count':len(rows), 'eligible_count':len(eligible),
            'selected_count':len(selected), 'limit':limit, 'limit_reached':len(eligible)>len(selected)}
        return selected

    def fetch_books_from_api(self, target_count=300):
        key = self._key('LIBRARY_AUTH_KEY','LIBRARY_NARU_API_KEY')
        cfg = self.config['library']
        base = cfg['base_url']
        if base.endswith('loanItemSrch'):
            base = urljoin(base, 'srchBooks')  # compatibility for pre-patch YAML
        keywords = self.config.get('ingestion',{}).get('book_keywords') or [
            '컴퓨터 비전','자연어 처리','생성형 AI','머신러닝','데이터베이스','파이썬','쿠버네티스','소프트웨어','정보보안']
        size = cfg.get('page_size',50)
        page_limit = min(self.max_pages, cfg.get('max_pages', self.max_pages))
        rows, seen, queries = [], set(), []
        for word in keywords:
            count, signatures = 0, set()
            for page in range(1, page_limit+1):
                try:
                    response = self._request(base, {'authKey':key,'title':word,'pageNo':page,
                        'pageSize':size,'format':'json'}).get('response',{})
                    if not isinstance(response,dict):raise SourceError('UNEXPECTED_RESPONSE_SCHEMA')
                    if 'error' in response:
                        # Classify errors without echoing API text that may contain a key.
                        message = str(response['error']).lower()
                        category = 'unspecified'
                        if any(s in message for s in ('초과','한도','quota','rate limit','too many')):
                            category = 'quota_or_rate_limit'
                        elif any(s in message for s in ('인증','auth','permission','키')):
                            category = 'authentication_or_permission'
                        raise SourceError('API_RESULT_ERROR',category=category)
                    docs = response.get('docs')
                    if not isinstance(docs,list): raise SourceError('UNEXPECTED_RESPONSE_SCHEMA')
                    signature=hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(docs,sort_keys=True).encode()).hexdigest()
                    if docs and signature in signatures:raise SourceError('PAGINATION_NOT_ADVANCING')
                except SourceError as exc:
                    exc.details.update(keyword=word,page=page)
                    self.reports['library']={'status':'FAILED','reason':str(exc),**exc.details,
                        'completed_queries':queries,'discarded_candidate_count':len(rows)}
                    raise
                signatures.add(signature);count+=len(docs)
                for wrapper in docs:
                    item=wrapper.get('doc',{})
                    isbn=text(item.get('isbn13') or item.get('isbn'))
                    if not isbn or not re.fullmatch(r'[0-9Xx-]{10,20}',isbn) or isbn in seen:continue
                    seen.add(isbn)
                    row=self._record('books','API_DATA4LIBRARY',isbn, {
                        'isbn':isbn,'title':text(item.get('bookname')),'author':text(item.get('authors')),
                        'publisher':text(item.get('publisher')),'loan_count':number(item.get('loan_count')),
                        'kdc_code':text(item.get('class_no'),10),'record_source':'API_DATA4LIBRARY'},item,
                        match_text=text(item.get('bookname')))
                    if row:rows.append(row)
                total=number(response.get('numFound'))
                complete=not docs or (total is not None and count>=total)
                if complete:break
            queries.append({'keyword':word,'pages_read':page,'raw_count':count,
                'reported_total':total,'page_limit_reached':not complete})
        self.reports['library']={'status':'OK','raw_count':sum(q['raw_count'] for q in queries),
            'queries':queries,'endpoint':'srchBooks','title_search':True,
            'page_limit_reached':any(q['page_limit_reached'] for q in queries),
            'unused_legacy_settings':['kdc_code'] if cfg.get('kdc_code') else []}
        detailed = catalog_details.enrich_books(self,self._select('books',rows,target_count))
        if self._library_blocked is not None:
            reason,details = self._library_blocked
            raise SourceError(reason,**details)
        return detailed

    def fetch_jobs_from_api(self, target_count=30):
        configured=self.config['careernet']['base_url']
        endpoint=self.config['careernet'].get('jobs_url') or (
            'https://www.career.go.kr/cnet/front/openapi/jobs.json'
            if configured.endswith('getOpenApi.json') else configured)
        items, seen = [], set()
        queries = self.config['careernet'].get('job_queries') or [
            {'searchAptdCodes':'104740'},{'searchThemeCode':'102420'},{'searchThemeCode':'102421'}]
        for i, query in enumerate(queries):
            fetched=self._pages('careernet_'+str(i),endpoint,
                {'apiKey':self._key('CAREERNET_API_KEY'),**query},
                page_key='pageIndex',size_key='pageSize',size=10)
            for item in fetched:
                if item.get('job_cd') not in seen:
                    seen.add(item.get('job_cd'));items.append(item)
        rows=[]
        for item in items:
            title=text(item.get('job_nm') or item.get('job'))
            if not title: continue
            key='jobs:'+str(item['job_cd']) if item.get('job_cd') is not None else None
            row=self._record('jobs','API_CAREERNET',key, {'title':title,
                # CareerNet summaries are not NCS competency units. Keep them in the raw audit only.
                'ncs_units':None,'record_source':'API_CAREERNET'},item,
                match_text=title+' '+(text(item.get('work')) or ''))
            if row:rows.append(row)
        return catalog_details.enrich_jobs(self,self._select('jobs',rows,target_count,include_unmatched=True))

    def fetch_certifications_from_api(self,target_count=30):
        items=self._pages('kdata',self.config['kdata']['base_url'],{'serviceKey':self._key('DATA_GO_KR_API_KEY')})
        groups={}
        for item in items:
            title=next((text(v) for k,v in item.items() if re.sub(r'[\sㆍ_·/()（）]','',k) in
                        ('자격증명','종목명','자격명','자격종목') and text(v)),None)
            if title:groups.setdefault(title,[]).append(item)
        rows=[]
        for title,variants in groups.items():
            subjects=sorted({text(v) for item in variants for k,v in item.items() if k.startswith('과목명') and text(v)})
            scores={number(i.get('합격점수')) for i in variants}
            types=sorted({text(i.get('필기실기구분')) for i in variants if text(i.get('필기실기구분'))})
            providers={text(i.get('시행기관') or i.get('발급기관')) for i in variants}
            row=self._record('certifications','API_ODCLOUD_KDATA',title, {
                'title':title,'provider':next(iter(providers)) if len(providers)==1 else None,
                'test_subjects':' / '.join(subjects) or None,'career_outlook':None,
                'exam_type':' / '.join(types) or None,
                'pass_score':next(iter(scores)) if len(scores)==1 else None,
                'record_source':'API_ODCLOUD_KDATA'},variants)
            if row:rows.append(row)
        try:
            rows.extend(catalog_details.microsoft_certifications(self))
        except Exception as exc:
            self.reports['ms_certifications']={'status':'FAILED','reason':type(exc).__name__}
        return self._select('certifications',rows,target_count,include_unmatched=True)

    def fetch_majors_from_api(self,target_count=60):
        items=self._pages('university',self.config['university']['base_url'],
                          {'serviceKey':self._key('DATA_GO_KR_API_KEY')},size=1000)
        groups={}
        for item in items:
            title=text(item.get('학부_과(전공)명') or item.get('학과명') or item.get('전공명'))
            if title and IT_TITLE.search(title):groups.setdefault(title,[]).append(item)
        rows=[]
        for title,variants in groups.items():
            # Major classification is not a curriculum. Preserve the raw source separately.
            row=self._record('majors','API_ODCLOUD_UNIVERSITY',title,{'title':title,'curriculum':None},variants,match_text=title)
            if row:rows.append(row)
        try:
            detailed=catalog_details.career_majors(self,IT_TITLE)
            # Prefer detailed canonical majors over same-title institution inventory rows.
            titles={r['fields']['title'] for r in detailed}
            rows=detailed+[r for r in rows if r['fields']['title'] not in titles]
        except Exception as exc:
            self.reports['careernet_majors']={'status':'FAILED','reason':type(exc).__name__}
        return self._select('majors',rows,target_count,include_unmatched=True)

    def collect(self,skills,*,max_pages=None,limits=None):
        if not skills:raise ValueError('기존 skill_tags 원본을 먼저 적재하세요. 스킬을 초기화하지 않습니다.')
        self.matcher=SkillMatcher(skills)
        settings=self.config.get('ingestion',{})
        self.max_pages=max_pages if max_pages is not None else settings.get('max_pages',50)
        if type(self.max_pages) is not int or not 1<=self.max_pages<=1000:
            raise ValueError('max_pages must be between 1 and 1000')
        self.reports={};self.selection_reports={};self._library_blocked=None
        limits=limits or {**LIMITS,**settings.get('limits',{})}
        if any(type(limits.get(k)) is not int or limits[k]<1 for k in KINDS):
            raise ValueError('All catalog limits must be positive integers')
        rows={};failures={}
        for kind in KINDS:
            method=getattr(self,'fetch_'+kind+'_from_api')
            try:
                rows[kind]=catalog_details.finalize(self,kind,method(limits[kind]))
                if not rows[kind]:failures[kind]='NO_MATCHED_ROWS'
                elif not any(r['fields']['content_status']=='ready' for r in rows[kind]):
                    failures[kind]='NO_RECOMMENDABLE_CONTENT'
            except SourceError as exc:
                rows[kind]=[];failures[kind]=str(exc)
                self.reports[kind+'_failure']={'status':'FAILED','reason':str(exc),**exc.details}
                logging.getLogger('LOAD_DATA').error('%s collection failed: %s',kind,str(exc))
            logging.getLogger('LOAD_DATA').info('%s: %s개 수집',kind,len(rows[kind]))
        return {'rows':rows,'failures':failures,'sources':self.reports,
                'skill_identity':skills,'unresolved_rule_names':self.matcher.unresolved_rules,
                'selection':self.selection_reports,
                'quality':{k:{'ready':sum(r['fields'].get('content_status')=='ready' for r in group),
                    'needs_enrichment':sum(r['fields'].get('content_status')!='ready' for r in group),
                    'missing_fields':dict(Counter(f for r in group for f in missing_fields(k,r['fields'])))}
                    for k,group in rows.items()},
                'collection_complete':not failures and not any(r.get('page_limit_reached') or r.get('status')=='FAILED' or r.get('failed',0)>0 or r.get('detail_failed',0)>0 for r in self.reports.values())
                    and not any(r['limit_reached'] for r in self.selection_reports.values())}

    def apply(self,conn,batch,*,embed=True,embedding_client=None,allow_partial=False):
        if batch['failures'] and not allow_partial:
            raise ValueError('API 수집 실패 또는 매칭 자료 없음: '+', '.join(batch['failures'])+'. 기존 DB는 변경하지 않았습니다.')
        if not any(batch['rows'].values()):raise ValueError('적재할 API 데이터가 없습니다.')
        with conn.transaction():
            conn.execute('SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(763250131)')
            current=conn.execute('SELECT skill_id,name,category FROM public.skill_tags ORDER BY skill_id').fetchall()
            if current!=batch['skill_identity']:raise ValueError('수집 중 스킬 태그가 변경되었습니다. 다시 실행하세요.')
            deployment.apply_ingestion_schema(conn)
            conn.execute(AUDIT_DDL)
            # Persist newly observed retirement notices for older rows, even though
            # the API adapter deliberately withholds those rows from new ingestion.
            for notice in batch['sources'].get('ms_certifications', {}).get('unavailable', []):
                if notice.get('status') not in ('retired','retiring','availability_needs_review'):
                    continue
                conn.execute("""UPDATE public.certifications c SET content_status=%s
                    FROM growpath_meta.catalog_api_sources s
                    WHERE s.resource_type='certifications' AND s.source='API_MS_CERT'
                      AND s.external_key=%s AND s.entity_key=c.cert_id::text""",
                    (notice['status'], notice['uid']))
            stats={}
            for kind in KINDS:
                source_records=batch['rows'][kind]
                records=[r for r in source_records if r['fields'].get('content_status')=='ready'
                         and exclusion_reason(kind,r['fields'],r['source'],r['raw'],r['fetched_at']) is None]
                if not records:continue
                pk,map_table=TABLES[kind]
                # Synchronize explicit integer-ID allocation with all table writers; keep existing IDs.
                conn.execute(f'LOCK TABLE public.{kind} IN SHARE ROW EXCLUSIVE MODE')
                projection = '*' if kind == 'certifications' else f'{pk},title'
                existing=conn.execute(f'SELECT {projection} FROM public.{kind}').fetchall()
                excluded_cert_ids = {old[pk] for old in existing if kind == 'certifications'
                    and (old.get('content_status') in ('retired','retiring','availability_needs_review')
                         or catalog_details.certification_availability(old) != 'available')}

                titles={}
                for old in existing:titles.setdefault(old['title'],[]).append(old[pk])
                next_id=max((r[pk] for r in existing),default=0)+1 if kind in ('jobs','majors','certifications') else None
                # No sequence mutation here; the CLI syncs it after successful embedding.
                registry={(r['source'],r['external_key']):r['entity_key'] for r in conn.execute(
                    'SELECT source,external_key,entity_key FROM growpath_meta.catalog_api_sources WHERE resource_type=%s',(kind,)).fetchall()}
                counts={'inserted':0,'updated':0,'mapping_links':0,'withheld_for_enrichment':len(source_records)-len(records)}
                old_ids={r[pk] for r in existing}
                used=set()
                for record in records:
                    fields=dict(record['fields']);key=(record['source'],record['external_key'])
                    entity_id=registry.get(key)
                    if kind in ('jobs','majors','certifications'):
                        if entity_id is not None:entity_id=int(entity_id)
                        else:
                            matches=titles.get(fields['title'],[])
                            if len(matches)>1:raise ValueError(f'{kind}: 같은 이름의 기존 ID가 여러 개입니다. 중복을 확인하세요.')
                            if matches:entity_id=matches[0]
                            else:entity_id=next_id;next_id+=1
                    else:
                        if entity_id is not None and entity_id!=fields[pk]:raise ValueError('원본 ID가 기존 출처와 다릅니다.')
                        entity_id=fields[pk]
                    if kind == 'certifications' and entity_id in excluded_cert_ids:
                        counts['withheld_retired_or_retiring'] = counts.get('withheld_retired_or_retiring', 0) + 1
                        continue
                    if entity_id in used:raise ValueError('API 원본 ID 충돌을 발견했습니다. 적재를 중단합니다.')
                    used.add(entity_id)
                    fields[pk]=entity_id
                    fields['last_batch_updated']=datetime.now(timezone.utc)
                    names=list(fields)
                    params=[fields[n] for n in names]
                    assignments=','.join(f'{n}=EXCLUDED.{n}' for n in names if n!=pk)
                    conn.execute(f'''INSERT INTO public.{kind} ({','.join(names)}) VALUES ({','.join(['%s']*len(names))})
                        ON CONFLICT ({pk}) DO UPDATE SET {assignments}''',params)
                    # Replace links only for refreshed records. Unfetched rows and their links remain intact.
                    conn.execute(f'DELETE FROM public.{map_table} WHERE {pk}=%s',(entity_id,))
                    for sid in record['matches']:
                        conn.execute(f'INSERT INTO public.{map_table} ({pk},skill_id) VALUES (%s,%s) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING',(entity_id,sid))
                    conn.execute('''INSERT INTO growpath_meta.catalog_api_sources
                        (resource_type,source,external_key,entity_key,payload,mapping_evidence,fetched_at)
                        VALUES (%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s) ON CONFLICT (resource_type,source,external_key)
                        DO UPDATE SET entity_key=EXCLUDED.entity_key,payload=EXCLUDED.payload,
                        mapping_evidence=EXCLUDED.mapping_evidence,fetched_at=EXCLUDED.fetched_at''',
                        (kind,*key,str(entity_id),Jsonb(record['raw']),Jsonb(record['matches']),record['fetched_at']))
                    counts['updated' if entity_id in old_ids else 'inserted']+=1
                    counts['mapping_links']+=len(record['matches'])
                stats[kind]=counts
            certification_status_changes=catalog_details.refresh_certification_status(conn)
            vector_result=vector_index.embed(conn,client=embedding_client) if embed else vector_index.status(conn)
            return {'status':'PARTIAL' if batch['failures'] else 'SUCCESS','tables':stats,
                    'sources':batch['sources'],'skipped':batch['failures'], 'vectors':vector_result,
                    'certification_status_changes':certification_status_changes,
                    'quality':batch.get('quality',{}),'selection':batch.get('selection',{}),'collection_complete':batch.get('collection_complete',False),
                    'skills_changed':False,'regulations_changed':False,'user_data_changed':False,
                    'vector_scope':'existing skill/course/book/certification index; existing input format preserved',
                    'database_changes':True,'original_embedding_columns_changed':False}

    def execute_full_pipeline(self,*,dry_run=False,max_pages=None,allow_partial=False,skip_embeddings=False,db_env='SUPABASE_DB_URL'):
        with deployment.connect(db_env) as conn:
            conn.execute('SET TRANSACTION READ ONLY')
            skills=conn.execute('SELECT skill_id,name,category FROM public.skill_tags ORDER BY skill_id').fetchall()
        batch=self.collect(skills,max_pages=max_pages)
        if batch['failures'] and not dry_run and not allow_partial:
            return {'status':'FAILED','error_type':'SourceError','database_changes':False,
                    'embedding_api_called':False,'message':'수집 실패로 저장을 시작하지 않았습니다. failures와 sources를 확인하세요.',
                    'counts':{k:len(v) for k,v in batch['rows'].items()},
                    'failures':batch['failures'],'sources':batch['sources'],
                    'quality':batch.get('quality',{}),'selection':batch.get('selection',{}),
                    'collection_complete':batch.get('collection_complete',False)}
        if dry_run:
            return {'status':'READY' if not batch['failures'] else 'INCOMPLETE','database_changes':False,
                    'embedding_api_called':False,'counts':{k:len(v) for k,v in batch['rows'].items()},
                    'sources':batch['sources'],'failures':batch['failures'],
                    'quality':batch.get('quality',{}),'selection':batch.get('selection',{}),'collection_complete':batch.get('collection_complete',False),
                    'unresolved_rule_names':batch['unresolved_rule_names'],
                    'skill_coverage':dict(Counter(sid for rows in batch['rows'].values() for r in rows for sid in r['matches']))}
        with deployment.connect(db_env) as conn:
            result=self.apply(conn,batch,embed=not skip_embeddings,allow_partial=allow_partial)
            # Advance the legacy serial sequence under the table lock only on actual application.
            # Tests call apply() in a rollback transaction and do not touch non-transactional sequences.
            if 'certifications' in result['tables']:
                seq=conn.execute("SELECT pg_get_serial_sequence('public.certifications','cert_id') AS name").fetchone()['name']
                if seq:
                    top=conn.execute('SELECT max(cert_id) AS id FROM public.certifications').fetchone()['id']
                    # nextval never moves backwards; do not lower a sequence already ahead of max(id).
                    current=conn.execute('SELECT nextval(%s::regclass) AS id',(seq,)).fetchone()['id']
                    if top and current<top:conn.execute('SELECT setval(%s::regclass,%s,true)',(seq,top))
        return result

    def clear_all_tables(self):
        raise RuntimeError('전체 삭제 기능을 제거했습니다. load_data.py로 증분 갱신하세요.')

    def ingest_and_map_all(self):
        return self.execute_full_pipeline()

    def map_skills_to_entities(self):
        raise RuntimeError('무근거 전체 재매핑을 중단했습니다. load_data.py로 API 원본과 함께 갱신하세요.')


real_data_ingestor=RealDataIngestor()
__all__=['RealDataIngestor','real_data_ingestor']
</file>

<file path="src/services/assessment_service.py">
# =====================================================================
# FILE: src/core/assessment_service.py
# =====================================================================

import logging
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

from src.db.db_client import db_client, DatabaseClient
from src.common.assessment_schema import validate_questions, validate_responses, summarize_assessment

logger = logging.getLogger("ASSESSMENT_SERVICE")


class AssessmentService:
    """
    1~2단계 프로젝트 계획, 대화 로그, 3/6단계 5점 자기보고 진단 전담 서비스.
    """

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.db = db_client

    def get_project_plan(self, session_id: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        return self.db.get_project_plan(session_id)

    def save_project_plan(self, session_id: str, plan_data: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
        return self.db.save_project_plan(session_id, plan_data)

    def get_plan_conversations(self, session_id: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        return self.db.get_plan_conversations(session_id)

    def save_plan_conversation(self, session_id: str, role: str, content: str, sequence_no: int) -> bool:
        return self.db.save_plan_conversation(session_id, role, content, sequence_no)

    def get_assessment(self, session_id: str, assessment_type: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """질문과 응답을 안전하게 조인하여 skill, score, selected_option을 일원화 반환"""
        questions = self.db.get_assessment_questions(session_id, assessment_type)
        responses = self.db.get_assessment_responses(session_id, assessment_type)

        q_dict = {q["question_id"]: q for q in questions}
        joined_responses = []

        for r in responses:
            qid = r.get("question_id")
            q_info = q_dict.get(qid, {})
            skill_name = q_info.get("skill", q_info.get("skill_name", r.get("skill", "General")))

            joined_responses.append({
                "response_id": r.get("response_id"),
                "question_id": qid,
                "skill_id": q_info.get("skill_id"),
                "skill": skill_name,
                "skill_name": skill_name,
                "question": q_info.get("question", ""),
                "options": q_info.get("options", []),
                "selected_option": r.get("selected_option", ""),
                "score": r.get("score"),
                "assessment_type": assessment_type
            })

        return {
            "questions": questions,
            "responses": joined_responses
        }

    def save_assessment_questions(self, session_id: str, assessment_type: str, questions: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> bool:
        validate_questions(questions, require_ids=False)
        catalog = {s["skill_id"]: s["name"] for s in self.db.list_skill_tags()}
        if any(catalog.get(q["skill_id"]) != q["skill"] for q in questions):
            raise ValueError("DB 스킬 ID와 이름이 일치하지 않습니다.")
        expected = {s["skill_id"] for s in self.project_skills(session_id)}
        if {q["skill_id"] for q in questions} != expected:
            raise ValueError("설문 스킬이 프로젝트의 DB 스킬 목록과 일치하지 않습니다.")
        if assessment_type == "POST":
            pre = self.db.get_assessment_questions(session_id, "PRE")
            validate_questions(pre)
            keys = ("sequence_no", "skill_id", "question", "options")
            if [[q[k] for k in keys] for q in pre] != [[q[k] for k in keys] for q in questions]:
                raise ValueError("사후 설문은 사전 설문과 동일한 문항이어야 합니다.")
        return self.db.save_assessment_questions(session_id, assessment_type, questions)

    def save_assessment_responses(self, session_id: str, assessment_type: str, responses: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> bool:
        questions = self.db.get_assessment_questions(session_id, assessment_type)
        validate_responses(questions, responses)
        return self.db.save_assessment_responses(session_id, assessment_type, responses)

    def project_skills(self, session_id):
        plan = self.get_project_plan(session_id)
        names = plan.get("tech_stack") if plan else None
        return self.resolve_skill_names(names)

    def resolve_skill_names(self, names):
        if not isinstance(names, list) or not names or any(not isinstance(n, str) for n in names):
            raise ValueError("저장된 프로젝트의 DB 스킬 목록이 없습니다.")
        catalog = {s["name"]: s for s in self.db.list_skill_tags()}
        unknown = [name for name in names if name not in catalog]
        if unknown:
            raise ValueError("DB에 없는 프로젝트 스킬입니다: " + ", ".join(unknown))
        return [catalog[name] for name in dict.fromkeys(names)]

    def save_pre_assessment_with_skills(self, session_id, skill_names, questions):
        """Repair an unanswered legacy assessment after an explicit DB tag selection."""
        skills = self.resolve_skill_names(skill_names)
        validate_questions(questions, require_ids=False)
        if {q["skill_id"] for q in questions} != {s["skill_id"] for s in skills}:
            raise ValueError("선택한 DB 스킬과 설문 문항이 일치하지 않습니다.")
        with self.db.transaction() as conn:
            bound = DatabaseClient(conn)
            # This checks stage, canonical names, and absence of responses under a session lock.
            bound.save_assessment_questions(session_id, "PRE", questions)
            bound.save_project_plan(session_id, {"tech_stack": [s["name"] for s in skills]})
        return True

    def get_assessment_summary(self, session_id, assessment_type="PRE"):
        data = self.get_assessment(session_id, assessment_type)
        result = summarize_assessment(data["questions"], data["responses"])
        result.update(session_id=session_id, assessment_type=assessment_type)
        return result

    def get_assessment_report(self, session_id: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        row = self.db.get_assessment_report(session_id)
        return row.get("report_content") if row else None

    def save_assessment_report(self, session_id: str, report_content: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
        return self.db.save_assessment_report(session_id, report_content)


assessment_service = AssessmentService()
__all__ = ["AssessmentService", "assessment_service"]
</file>

<file path="src/services/prompt_manager.py">
"""
프롬프트 버전 관리 및 롤백 제어 모듈
파일 경로: src/services/prompt_manager.py
역할: DB prompt_versions 조회, 신규 버전 승격, 부모 버전 롤백, 인메모리 프롬프트 동기화
"""

import logging
from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional
from src.db.db_client import db_client

logger = logging.getLogger("PROMPT_MANAGER")


class PromptManager:
    """에이전트별 시스템 프롬프트 버전 및 수명주기 관리자"""

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.db = db_client

    def get_active_prompt(self, agent_id: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """에이전트의 현재 활성 프롬프트 조회"""
        return self.db.get_active_prompt_for_agent(agent_id)

    def get_all_active_prompts(self) -> Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]:
        """전체 에이전트 활성 프롬프트 맵 조회"""
        return self.db.get_active_prompts()

    def get_history(self, agent_id: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """에이전트의 버전 이력 조회"""
        return self.db.get_prompt_history(agent_id)

    def promote_candidate_prompt(
        self,
        agent_id: str,
        candidate_text: str,
        change_reason: str,
        parent_version_id: str
    ) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """
        가드레일 평가를 통과한 후보 프롬프트를 신규 버전으로 승격 및 활성화
        """
        history = self.get_history(agent_id)
        next_version = 1
        if history:
            next_version = max(row.get("version_num", 1) for row in history) + 1

        logger.info(f"[{agent_id}] 새 버전(v{next_version}) 등록 및 활성화 진행...")

        created = self.db.create_prompt_version(
            agent_id=agent_id,
            version_num=next_version,
            system_prompt=candidate_text,
            change_reason=change_reason,
            parent_version_id=parent_version_id,
            is_active=False
        )

        if not created:
            logger.error(f"[{agent_id}] 프롬프트 버전 레코드 생성 실패")
            return None

        new_vid = created["prompt_version_id"]

        success = self.db.activate_prompt_version(new_vid, agent_id)
        if success:
            logger.info(f"[{agent_id}] 신규 프롬프트 v{next_version} 활성화 성공 (ID: {new_vid})")
            created["is_active"] = True
            return created
        else:
            logger.error(f"[{agent_id}] 신규 프롬프트 활성화 전환 실패")
            return None

    def rollback(self, agent_id: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        현재 버전에 품질 이상 발생 시 직전 부모 버전으로 원클릭 롤백
        """
        logger.warning(f"[{agent_id}] 프롬프트 롤백 요청 수신...")
        return self.db.rollback_prompt_version(agent_id)


prompt_manager = PromptManager()
</file>

<file path="src/services/recommendation_service.py">
"""Vector rank within real DB skill-resource relationships, never a global fallback."""
import logging
import math
from contextlib import nullcontext
from urllib.parse import urlsplit

from src.db import resource_vectors as vectors
from src.common.catalog_quality import certification_availability
from src.common.catalog_eligibility import eligible_keys
from src.db.db_client import serializable

logger = logging.getLogger('RECOMMENDATION_SERVICE')
LIMITS = {'courses': 12, 'certifications': 6, 'books': 6}
MIN_SIMILARITY = 0.20  # Initial retrieval policy; evaluate recall before changing.


def normalize_course_url(raw_url, title='', platform=''):
    """Keep only actual absolute HTTP(S) URLs. Never invent a vendor or homepage."""
    if not isinstance(raw_url, str):
        return None
    value = raw_url.strip()
    try:
        parsed = urlsplit(value)
        if parsed.scheme in ('https', 'http') and parsed.hostname and not parsed.username and not parsed.password:
            return value
    except ValueError:
        pass
    return None


class RecommendationService:
    normalize_course_url = staticmethod(normalize_course_url)

    def retrieve_grounding_context(self, target_skills, target_job_title='',
                                   assessment_summary=None, project_summary='', *, conn=None):
        try:
            with (nullcontext(conn) if conn is not None else vectors.connect('SUPABASE_DB_URL')) as connection:
                if conn is None:
                    connection.execute('SET TRANSACTION ISOLATION LEVEL REPEATABLE READ READ ONLY')
                return self._retrieve(connection, target_skills, target_job_title,
                                      assessment_summary, project_summary)
        except ValueError:
            raise
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.error('학습 자료 검색 실패 (%s)', type(exc).__name__)
            raise RuntimeError('학습 자료 검색 실패: DB 연결 및 임베딩 API 상태를 확인하세요. 결과를 저장하지 않았습니다.') from None

    def _retrieve(self, conn, target_skills, job_title, assessment, project):
        rows = vectors.sources(conn)
        skills = {v['record']['name']: v['record'] for k, v in rows.items() if k[0] == 'skills'}
        if (not isinstance(target_skills, list) or not target_skills
                or any(not isinstance(s, str) or s not in skills for s in target_skills)):
            raise ValueError('목표 스킬을 DB의 실제 스킬 태그로 선택하세요.')
        targets = {skills[name]['skill_id']: skills[name] for name in target_skills}
        scores = {}
        if assessment is not None:
            if (assessment.get('schema_version') != 'skill_self_report_v1'
                    or assessment.get('assessment_method') != 'self_report'):
                raise ValueError('저장된 5점 자기보고 진단 입력이 필요합니다.')
            for item in assessment.get('skills', []):
                sid, value = item.get('skill_id'), item.get('mean_score')
                if (type(sid) is not int or sid not in targets or sid in scores
                        or item.get('skill') != targets[sid]['name']
                        or type(value) not in (int, float) or not math.isfinite(value) or not 1 <= value <= 5):
                    raise ValueError('진단 스킬/점수와 DB 목표 스킬이 일치하지 않습니다.')
                scores[sid] = value
            if set(scores) != set(targets):
                raise ValueError('목표 스킬의 진단 결과가 누락되었습니다.')
        skill_query = '\n'.join(targets[i]['name'] for i in sorted(targets))
        query = f'직무: {job_title}\n프로젝트: {project}\n학습 목표 스킬:\n{skill_query}'
        # Check provenance before paying for a query embedding.
        usable, eligibility_report = eligible_keys(conn)
        previous = vectors.stored(conn)
        relevant = {k: v for k, v in rows.items() if
                    (k[0] == 'skills' and v['record']['skill_id'] in targets) or
                    (k[0] != 'skills' and k in usable and set(v['record']['linked_skill_ids']) & set(targets)
                     and (k[0] != 'certifications' or (v['record'].get('content_status') == 'ready'
                         and len(str(v['record'].get('test_subjects') or '').strip()) >= 20
                         and certification_availability(v['record']) == 'available')))}
        if any(not vectors.is_current(v, previous.get(k)) for k, v in relevant.items()):
            raise ValueError('학습 자원 벡터가 없거나 오래되었습니다. python -m src.db.resource_vectors embed 를 실행하세요.')
        query_vector = vectors.embed_texts([query])[0]
        similarities = vectors.similarities(conn, rows, relevant, query_vector)
        ranked_skills = []
        for sid, skill in targets.items():
            sim = similarities[('skills', str(sid))]
            # A self-report gap is a prioritization hint, not an objective proficiency grade.
            gap = (5 - scores[sid]) / 4 if sid in scores else 0
            ranked_skills.append({'skill_id': sid, 'name': skill['name'], 'similarity': sim,
                                  'self_report_mean': scores.get(sid), 'priority': 0.8 * sim + 0.2 * gap})
        ranked_skills.sort(key=lambda s: (-s['priority'], s['skill_id']))
        context = {'target_job': job_title, 'target_skills': list(dict.fromkeys(target_skills)),
                   'job_profile': None, 'courses': [], 'certifications': [], 'books': []}
        coverage = {sid: {} for sid in targets}
        for kind, limit in LIMITS.items():
            candidates = []
            for key, source in relevant.items():
                if key[0] != kind:
                    continue
                row = serializable(source['record'])
                matched = sorted(set(row['linked_skill_ids']) & set(targets))
                for sid in matched:
                    coverage[sid][kind] = coverage[sid].get(kind, 0) + 1
                sim = similarities[key]
                if sim < MIN_SIMILARITY:
                    continue
                gap = max(((5 - scores[sid]) / 4 if sid in scores else 0) for sid in matched)
                row['matched_skill_ids'] = matched
                row['retrieval'] = {'similarity': sim, 'ranking_score': 0.8 * sim + 0.2 * gap,
                                    'source_sha256': source['hash']}
                if kind == 'courses':
                    row['source_url'] = row.get('url')
                    row['url'] = normalize_course_url(row.get('url'))
                    row['url_status'] = 'db_absolute_url' if row['url'] else 'missing_or_non_absolute'
                candidates.append(row)
            pk = vectors.SPECS[kind][1]
            candidates.sort(key=lambda r: (-r['retrieval']['ranking_score'], str(r[pk])))
            # Cover priority skills once before filling remaining slots by vector rank.
            picked, picked_ids = [], set()
            for skill in ranked_skills:
                if any(skill['skill_id'] in r['matched_skill_ids'] for r in picked):
                    continue
                candidate = next((r for r in candidates if skill['skill_id'] in r['matched_skill_ids']), None)
                if candidate and len(picked) < limit:
                    picked.append(candidate)
                    picked_ids.add(str(candidate[pk]))
            for row in candidates:
                if len(picked) == limit:
                    break
                if str(row[pk]) not in picked_ids:
                    picked.append(row)
                    picked_ids.add(str(row[pk]))
            context[kind] = picked
        if job_title:
            job = conn.execute('SELECT job_id,title,ncs_units FROM public.jobs WHERE title=%s ORDER BY job_id LIMIT 1',
                               (job_title,)).fetchone()
            context['job_profile'] = dict(job) if job else None
        coverage_rows = []
        for sid, skill in targets.items():
            coverage_rows.append({'skill_id': sid, 'name': skill['name'],
                'linked_counts': {kind: coverage[sid].get(kind, 0) for kind in LIMITS},
                'retrieved_counts': {kind: sum(sid in r['matched_skill_ids'] for r in context[kind]) for kind in LIMITS}})
        context['resource_rag'] = {
            'schema_version': 'db_resource_rag_v1', 'embedding_model': vectors.MODEL,
            'dimensions': vectors.DIMENSIONS, 'input_version': vectors.INPUT_VERSION,
            'query': query, 'matched_skills': ranked_skills, 'coverage': coverage_rows,
            'limits': LIMITS, 'minimum_similarity': MIN_SIMILARITY,
            'ranking_policy': '0.8*cosine_similarity + 0.2*self_report_gap; priority-skill coverage first',
            'no_evidence': not any(context[kind] for kind in LIMITS),
            'legacy_vectors_used': False,
            'availability_filter': eligibility_report,
        }
        logger.info('RAG: courses=%s certifications=%s books=%s', *(len(context[k]) for k in LIMITS))
        return context


recommendation_service = RecommendationService()
__all__ = ['RecommendationService', 'recommendation_service', 'normalize_course_url']
</file>

<file path="src/services/selection_service.py">
# =====================================================================
# FILE: src/core/selection_service.py
# =====================================================================

import logging
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

from src.db.db_client import db_client

logger = logging.getLogger("SELECTION_SERVICE")


class SelectionService:
    """
    프로젝트 로드맵 세션의 라이프사이클 및 4~5단계 대안/채택 트랜잭션을 전담하는 서비스 계층.
    """

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.db = db_client

    def create_roadmap_session(
        self,
        user_id: Optional[int] = None,
        project_name: Optional[str] = None,
        user_type: str = "REAL_USER",
        *args,
        **kwargs
    ) -> Optional[str]:
        """
        신규 프로젝트 세션 엔티티 생성.
        - test_step4_agents.py의 유효성 검증(user_type="INVALID_TYPE" 시 ValueError)
        - app.py의 신규 세션 발급(user_id, project_name)
        양쪽 호출 형태를 모두 지원합니다.
        """
        # 1. user_type 검증 (test_step4_agents.py 대응)
        target_utype = kwargs.get("user_type", user_type)
        if target_utype not in ("REAL_USER", "TEST_MANUAL", "TEST_AUTO"):
            raise ValueError(f"유효하지 않은 user_type 입니다: {target_utype}")

        # user_id is public.users.user_id; a profile UUID is not a user ID.
        target_uid = user_id if user_id is not None else kwargs.get("user_id")
        if target_uid is None:
            target_uid = kwargs.get("user_profile", {}).get("user_id")
        if target_uid is None:
            raise ValueError("DB에 등록된 사용자를 선택해 주세요.")

        # 3. project_name 추출 (프롬프트/프로필 요약 폴백)
        target_pname = (
            project_name
            or kwargs.get("project_name")
            or kwargs.get("raw_user_prompt")
            or kwargs.get("user_profile", {}).get("work_context_summary")
            or "신규 엔지니어링 프로젝트"
        )
        if isinstance(target_pname, str):
            target_pname = target_pname[:100]

        return self.db.create_roadmap_session(
            user_id=target_uid,
            project_name=target_pname,
            status="DRAFT",
            current_stage=1
        )

    def get_roadmap_session(self, session_id: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """DB에서 프로젝트 세션 조회"""
        return self.db.get_roadmap_session(session_id)

    def list_roadmap_sessions(self, user_id: Optional[int] = None, limit: int = 10) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """진행 중인 세션 목록 조회"""
        return self.db.list_roadmap_sessions(user_id=user_id, limit=limit)

    def update_session_stage(self, session_id: str, stage_num: int, status: str = "IN_PROGRESS") -> bool:
        """세션 단계 전이 갱신"""
        return self.db.update_session_stage(session_id, stage_num, status)

    def save_agent_generations(self, session_id: str, generations: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> bool:
        """[Stage 4] 3개 대안 로드맵 DB 일괄 저장"""
        return self.db.save_roadmap_generations(session_id, generations)

    def get_agent_generations(self, session_id: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """[Stage 4] DB에 저장된 3개 대안 로드맵 조회"""
        return self.db.get_roadmap_generations(session_id)

    def select_roadmap(self, session_id: str, generation_id: str, detailed_report: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
        """[Stage 5] 선택 결과 및 상세 설계 보고서 원자적 영속화"""
        return self.db.select_roadmap_with_report(session_id, generation_id, detailed_report)

    def get_selected_roadmap(self, session_id: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """[Stage 5] 선택된 로드맵 및 설계 보고서 조회"""
        sel = self.db.get_roadmap_selection(session_id)
        rep = self.db.get_roadmap_report(session_id)
        if not sel:
            return None

        gen_id = sel.get("generation_id")
        gens = self.db.get_roadmap_generations(session_id)
        chosen_gen = next((g for g in gens if g.get("generation_id") == gen_id), {})

        return {
            "selection": sel,
            "generation": chosen_gen,
            "report": rep.get("report_content") if rep else {}
        }

    # 레거시 하위 호환 메서드
    def record_agent_generations(self, request_id: str, orchestration_results: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, str]:
        gen_list = []
        for aid, res in orchestration_results.items():
            if res.get("status") != "SUCCESS":
                raise ValueError("실패한 생성 결과는 저장할 수 없습니다.")
            content = res.get("roadmap_content", {})
            gen_list.append({
                "strategy_type": aid,
                "strategy_title": content.get("strategy_title", aid),
                "summary": res.get("summary", ""),
                "roadmap_content": content
            })
        self.save_agent_generations(request_id, gen_list)
        return {row["strategy_type"]: row["generation_id"] for row in self.db.get_roadmap_generations(request_id)}

    def record_user_selection(self, request_id: str, chosen_generation_id: str, chosen_agent_id: str, user_type: str, selection_reason: Optional[str] = None) -> Optional[str]:
        self.db.save_roadmap_selection(request_id, chosen_generation_id)
        return chosen_generation_id

    def get_dashboard_summary(self, user_type: Optional[str] = "REAL_USER") -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {"statistics": {}, "trigger_status": {}}


selection_service = SelectionService()
__all__ = ["SelectionService", "selection_service"]

<file path="tests/run_all_tests.py">
import sys
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path

tests_dir = Path(__file__).resolve().parent

test_steps = [
    ("Step 1: 경로 및 YAML 설정 검증", tests_dir / "test_step1_config.py"),
    ("Step 2: DB 클라이언트 및 스키마 검증", tests_dir / "test_step2_db.py"),
    ("Step 3: 코어 컴포넌트(PII, 벡터, 서킷) 검증", tests_dir / "test_step3_core.py"),
    ("Step 4: 에이전트 및 파이프라인 검증", tests_dir / "test_step4_agents.py"),
]

def run_suite():
    print("==================================================")
    print("   GrowPath AI 프로젝트 단계별 무결성 진단 시작   ")
    print("==================================================\n")

    failed = False
    for step_name, script_path in test_steps:
        print(f"▶ 실행 중: {step_name} ({script_path.name})")
        res = subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(script_path)], capture_output=False)
        if res.returncode != 0:
            print(f"❌ [FAIL] {step_name}에서 오류가 발생했습니다. 해당 단계를 점검하세요.\n")
            failed = True
            break
        print("-" * 50)

    if not failed:
        print("\n🎉 모든 단계별 테스트를 통과했습니다! 프로젝트 구조가 완전히 정상입니다.")
        sys.exit(0)
    else:
        sys.exit(1)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    run_suite()

<file path="tests/test_db_deployment.py">
"""Dependency-free deployment checks; no DB, API, or synthetic knowledge is created."""
import json
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch

sys.path.insert(0,str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))
from src.db import deployment as db


class DeploymentTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_unknown_vector_backup_readable_but_import_blocked_before_db(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(dir=Path(__file__).resolve().parent) as folder:
            directory = Path(folder)
            manifest = self.bundle(directory)
            path = directory/'skills.jsonl'
            path.write_text(json.dumps({**self.skill(),'embedding':[0.1]*1536})+'\n',encoding='utf-8')
            manifest.update(format_version=2,embedding_model=None,embedding_model_status='unknown')
            manifest['tables'][0]['sha256'] = db.digest(path)
            (directory/'manifest.json').write_text(json.dumps(manifest),encoding='utf-8')
            _,rows = db.read_bundle(directory)
            self.assertEqual(len(rows['public.skill_tags']),1)
            with patch.object(db,'migrate') as migrate:
                with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError,'provenance is unknown'):
                    db.bootstrap(None,directory)
                migrate.assert_not_called()
            with patch.object(db,'connect') as connect:
                with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError,'provenance is unknown'):
                    db.main(['--db-env','TARGET_DB_URL','bootstrap','--input',str(directory)])
                connect.assert_not_called()

    def test_unknown_model_without_vectors_can_import(self):
        db.require_import_provenance({'embedding_model':None},{'public.skill_tags':[self.skill()]})

    def test_placeholder_model_rejected_before_connect(self):
        with patch.object(db,'connect') as connect:
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError,'verified embedding model'):
                db.main(['export-data','--output','unused','--embedding-model','ACTUAL_EMBEDDING_MODEL'])
            connect.assert_not_called()

    def test_export_without_model_or_with_empty_shell_argument(self):
        from contextlib import redirect_stdout
        from io import StringIO
        for suffix in ([],['--embedding-model'],['--embedding-model','']):
            with patch.object(db,'connect') as connect, patch.object(db,'export_data',return_value={}) as export:
                with redirect_stdout(StringIO()):
                    db.main(['export-data','--output','unused']+suffix)
                connect.assert_called_once_with('SUPABASE_DB_URL')
                self.assertIsNone(export.call_args.args[2])

    def skill(self):
        return {'skill_id':1,'name':'SQL 기본 문법 & 조인','category':'데이터베이스','embedding':None}

    def bundle(self, directory):
        path = directory/'skills.jsonl'
        path.write_text(json.dumps(self.skill(),ensure_ascii=False)+'\n',encoding='utf-8')
        manifest = {'format_version':1,'kind':'database_export','embedding_model':'verified-test-model',
                    'tables':[{'table':'public.skill_tags','file':path.name,'rows':1,'sha256':db.digest(path)}]}
        (directory/'manifest.json').write_text(json.dumps(manifest),encoding='utf-8')
        return manifest

    def test_foreign_key_order(self):
        order = db.table_order(db.CATALOG)
        self.assertEqual(len(order),42)
        position = {t:i for i,t in enumerate(order)}
        for table, spec in db.CATALOG.items():
            for fk in spec['fks']:
                self.assertIn(fk['target'],db.CATALOG)
                if fk['target'] != table:
                    self.assertLess(position[fk['target']],position[table])

    def test_real_regulation_integrity_and_foreign_keys(self):
        tables = db.package_rows(db.PILOT)
        self.assertEqual(len(tables['hr_regulation_pilot.regulation_node']),202)
        self.assertEqual(len(tables['hr_regulation_pilot.rag_chunk']),106)
        for table, rows in tables.items():
            for fk in db.CATALOG[table]['fks']:
                targets = {tuple(r[c] for c in fk['target_columns']) for r in tables[fk['target']]}
                for row in rows:
                    key = tuple(row[c] for c in fk['columns'])
                    if all(v is not None for v in key):
                        self.assertIn(key,targets)

    def test_self_parent_before_child(self):
        table = 'hr_regulation_pilot.regulation_node'
        ordered = db.row_order(table,list(reversed(db.package_rows(db.PILOT)[table])))
        seen = set()
        for row in ordered:
            if row['parent_node_id']:
                self.assertIn(row['parent_node_id'],seen)
            seen.add(row['regulation_node_id'])

    def test_parent_cycle_rejected(self):
        rows = [{'regulation_node_id':'a','parent_node_id':'b'}, {'regulation_node_id':'b','parent_node_id':'a'}]
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError,'cycle'):
            db.row_order('hr_regulation_pilot.regulation_node',rows)

    def test_id_or_name_reassignment_rejected(self):
        original = self.skill()
        for changed in ({**original,'name':'new'}, {**original,'skill_id':9}, {**original,'category':'new'}):
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError,'conflict'):
                db.validate_skill_identity([original],[changed])
        db.validate_skill_identity([original],[original])

    def test_wrong_vector_dimension_and_nonfinite_rejected(self):
        for vector in ([1.0]*3, [float('nan')]*1536, '[1,2]'):
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError,'vector'):
                db.validate_rows('public.skill_tags',[{**self.skill(),'embedding':vector}])

    def test_missing_column_and_duplicate_pk_rejected(self):
        bad = self.skill()
        del bad['name']
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError,'Column set'):
            db.validate_rows('public.skill_tags',[bad])
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError,'Duplicate'):
            db.validate_rows('public.skill_tags',[self.skill(),self.skill()])

    def test_bundle_and_checksum(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(dir=Path(__file__).resolve().parent) as folder:
            directory = Path(folder)
            self.assertTrue(directory.resolve().is_relative_to(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
            self.bundle(directory)
            _, rows = db.read_bundle(directory)
            self.assertEqual(rows['public.skill_tags'][0],self.skill())
            (directory/'skills.jsonl').write_text('{}',encoding='utf-8')
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError,'Checksum'):
                db.read_bundle(directory)

    def test_manifest_traversal_and_unknown_table_rejected(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(dir=Path(__file__).resolve().parent) as folder:
            directory = Path(folder)
            self.assertTrue(directory.resolve().is_relative_to(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
            manifest = self.bundle(directory)
            manifest['tables'][0]['file'] = '../outside.jsonl'
            (directory/'manifest.json').write_text(json.dumps(manifest),encoding='utf-8')
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError,'escapes'):
                db.read_bundle(directory)
            manifest['tables'][0]['table'] = 'auth.users'
            (directory/'manifest.json').write_text(json.dumps(manifest),encoding='utf-8')
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError,'Unsupported'):
                db.read_bundle(directory)

    def test_migrations_repeat_without_reexecution_and_checksum_drift_fails(self):
        class Result:
            def __init__(self, rows=()): self.rows=rows
            def fetchall(self): return self.rows
        class Connection:
            def __init__(self): self.applied={}; self.sql=[]
            def execute(self, sql, params=None, **kwargs):
                self.sql.append(sql)
                if sql.startswith('SELECT version,sha256'):
                    return Result([{'version':k,'sha256':v} for k,v in self.applied.items()])
                if sql.startswith('INSERT INTO growpath_meta.schema_migrations'):
                    self.applied[params[0]]=params[1]
                return Result()
        conn = Connection()
        with patch.object(db,'check_schema'):
            first = db.migrate(conn)
            second = db.migrate(conn)
            self.assertEqual(len(first['applied']),4)
            self.assertEqual(second['applied'],[])
            conn.applied[first['applied'][0]] = 'modified'
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError,'migration changed'):
                db.migrate(conn)

    def test_bootstrap_requires_actual_skills_before_migration(self):
        with patch.object(db,'read_bundle',return_value=({},{})), patch.object(db,'migrate') as migrate:
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError,'real skill_tags'):
                db.bootstrap(None,Path('.'))
            migrate.assert_not_called()

    def test_bootstrap_rejects_partial_regulation_bundle_before_migration(self):
        tables = {'public.skill_tags':[self.skill()], 'hr_regulation_pilot.institution':[]}
        with patch.object(db,'read_bundle',return_value=({},tables)), patch.object(db,'migrate') as migrate:
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError,'Partial regulation'):
                db.bootstrap(None,Path('.'))
            migrate.assert_not_called()


if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main(verbosity=2)

<file path="tests/test_project_structure.py">
"""Offline checks for the structure-only migration; no application imports."""
import ast
import csv
import json
import runpy
import sys
import types
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]


class ProjectStructureTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_python_syntax_and_internal_import_targets(self):
        for file in [ROOT / 'app.py', ROOT / 'load_data.py', ROOT / 'reset_skills.py',
                     *ROOT.joinpath('src').rglob('*.py'), *ROOT.joinpath('tests').glob('*.py')]:
            with self.subTest(file=str(file.relative_to(ROOT))):
                tree = ast.parse(file.read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig'))
                for node in ast.walk(tree):
                    if not isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom) or not node.module:
                        continue
                    self.assertFalse(node.module.startswith('src.core'))
                    self.assertFalse(node.module.startswith('db.'))
                    if node.module.startswith('src.'):
                        module = ROOT.joinpath(*node.module.split('.'))
                        self.assertTrue(module.with_suffix('.py').exists() or (module/'__init__.py').exists(), node.module)

    def test_config_and_export_root_resolution(self):
        config = ROOT / 'src/common/config_loader.py'
        export = ROOT / 'src/pipelines/export_finetuning_dataset.py'
        self.assertEqual(config.resolve().parent.parent.parent, ROOT)
        self.assertEqual(export.resolve().parent.parent.parent, ROOT)
        for name in ('paths.yaml', 'rules.yaml'):
            self.assertTrue((ROOT / 'config' / name).is_file())
        self.assertTrue((ROOT / 'data/processed').is_dir())
        self.assertTrue((ROOT / 'data/exports').is_dir())

    def test_actual_skill_snapshot_preserved(self):
        with (ROOT/'data/reference/skill_tags.csv').open(encoding='utf-8-sig', newline='') as f:
            rows = [{**r, 'skill_id': int(r['skill_id'])} for r in csv.DictReader(f)]
        tree = ast.parse((ROOT/'reset_skills.py').read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig'))
        declared = next(ast.literal_eval(n.value) for n in tree.body if isinstance(n, ast.Assign)
                        and any(isinstance(t, ast.Name) and t.id == 'STANDARD_SKILLS' for t in n.targets))
        self.assertEqual(rows, declared)
        self.assertEqual(len(rows), 36)

    def test_regulation_files_and_source_paths(self):
        package = ROOT/'data/regulations/인재개발규정_시범_V01'
        schema = json.loads((package/'schema.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig'))
        self.assertEqual(len(schema['tables']), 9)
        for table in schema['tables']:
            self.assertTrue((package/'data'/f'{table}.json').is_file())
        files = json.loads((package/'data/regulation_file.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig'))
        for row in files:
            self.assertTrue((package/row['storage_key']).is_file(), row['storage_key'])
        for name in ('01_schema.sql', '02_data.sql'):
            self.assertTrue((package/'sql'/name).is_file())

    def test_legacy_export_entry_dispatches_without_running_export(self):
        # Replace only the export implementation to test command routing without DB/API calls.
        stub = types.ModuleType('src.pipelines.export_finetuning_dataset')
        calls = []
        stub.main = lambda: calls.append('main')
        stub.GrowPathFlawlessExporter = object
        stub.InMemoryVectorStore = object
        previous_path = list(sys.path)
        try:
            with patch.dict(sys.modules, {stub.__name__: stub}):
                runpy.run_path(str(ROOT/'src/processed/export_finetuning_dataset.py'), run_name='__main__')
            self.assertEqual(calls, ['main'])
        finally:
            sys.path[:] = previous_path


if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main(verbosity=2)

import os
import sys
from pathlib import Path

# 프로젝트 루트 경로를 sys.path에 등록
project_root = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
if str(project_root) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(project_root))

from src.common.config_loader import PROJECT_ROOT, resolve_project_path


def test_project_root_detection():
    """PROJECT_ROOT가 실제 프로젝트 최상위를 가리키는지 확인"""
    assert PROJECT_ROOT.exists(), f"PROJECT_ROOT 경로가 존재하지 않습니다: {PROJECT_ROOT}"
    assert (PROJECT_ROOT / "src").exists(), f"src 디렉터리를 찾을 수 없습니다: {PROJECT_ROOT / 'src'}"
    print("[PASS] 1-1. PROJECT_ROOT 자동 감지 정상")


def test_config_directory_and_files():
    """config 디렉터리 및 필수 파일 접근성 확인"""
    config_dir = resolve_project_path("config")
    assert config_dir.exists(), f"config 디렉터리가 존재하지 않습니다: {config_dir}"

    paths_file = config_dir / "paths.yaml"
    rules_file = config_dir / "rules.yaml"

    if paths_file.exists():

        paths_cfg = get_paths_config()

    else:
        print("[WARN] 1-2. paths.yaml 파일이 아직 생성되지 않았습니다 (건너뜀).")

    if rules_file.exists():
        from src.common.config_loader import get_rules_config
        rules_cfg = get_rules_config()
        assert isinstance(rules_cfg, dict), "rules.yaml 파싱 결과가 dict 형태가 아닙니다."
        print("[PASS] 1-3. rules.yaml 파싱 정상")
    else:
        print("[WARN] 1-3. rules.yaml 파일이 아직 생성되지 않았습니다 (건너뜀).")


if __name__ == "__main__":

    print("=== [Step 1] 검증 완료 ===\n")

import os
import sys
from pathlib import Path

project_root = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
if str(project_root) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(project_root))

from src.db.db_client import db_client


def test_schema_file_exists():
    """통합 schema.sql 파일 존재 및 DDL 구문 로드 확인"""
    schema_path = project_root / "src" / "db" / "schema.sql"
    assert schema_path.exists(), f"통합 스키마 파일이 존재하지 않습니다: {schema_path}"

    with open(schema_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        sql_content = f.read()

    assert "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS skill_tags" in sql_content, "skill_tags 테이블 DDL 누락"
    assert "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS courses" in sql_content, "courses 테이블 DDL 누락"
    assert "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS prompt_versions" in sql_content, "prompt_versions 테이블 DDL 누락"
    assert "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users" in sql_content, "users 테이블(진단평가) DDL 누락"
    print("[PASS] 2-1. 통합 schema.sql 무결성 확인 완료")


def test_database_client_health():
    """데이터베이스 클라이언트 헬스체크 및 연결 상태 진단"""
    health = db_client.check_health()
    assert isinstance(health, dict), "check_health 반환값이 dict가 아닙니다."

    if health.get("connected"):
        print(f"[PASS] 2-2. DB 연결 성공: {health.get('message')}")
    else:
        print(f"[INFO] 2-2. DB 오프라인 상태 (환경변수 SUPABASE_URL / KEY 미설정 시 정상): {health.get('message')}")


def test_mock_profile_fallback():
    """DB 미연결 시 모의 프로필 조회 폴백 동작 확인"""
    profiles = db_client.fetch_mock_profiles()
    assert isinstance(profiles, list), "fetch_mock_profiles 반환값이 list가 아닙니다."
    print(f"[PASS] 2-3. 모의 프로필 인출 루틴 정상 (인출 건수: {len(profiles)}건)")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    print("=== [Step 2] DB 클라이언트 및 스키마 검증 시작 ===")
    test_schema_file_exists()
    test_database_client_health()
    test_mock_profile_fallback()

<file path="tests/test_step3_core.py">
import sys
from pathlib import Path

project_root = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
if str(project_root) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(project_root))

from src.common.pii_masking import pii_masker
from src.clients.embedding_service import embedding_service
from src.clients.llm_adapter import llm_adapter, CircuitState


def test_pii_masking():
    """개인식별정보(이름, 이메일, 전화번호, 주민번호 등) 마스킹 검증"""
    sample_text = "홍길동 책임연구원의 연락처는 010-1234-5678이며 이메일은 test@sk.com 입니다."
    masked = pii_masker.mask_text(sample_text)

    assert "010-1234-5678" not in masked, "전화번호가 마스킹되지 않았습니다."
    assert "test@sk.com" not in masked, "이메일이 마스킹되지 않았습니다."
    assert "[PHONE]" in masked, "[PHONE] 태그 누락"
    assert "[EMAIL]" in masked, "[EMAIL] 태그 누락"
    print("[PASS] 3-1. PII 마스킹 정규표현식 정상 작동")


def test_cosine_similarity_computation():
    """임베딩 서비스의 코사인 유사도 수학적 연산 정합성 검증"""
    vec_a = [1.0, 0.0, 0.0]
    vec_b = [1.0, 0.0, 0.0]
    vec_c = [0.0, 1.0, 0.0]

    sim_identical = embedding_service.cosine_similarity(vec_a, vec_b)
    sim_orthogonal = embedding_service.cosine_similarity(vec_a, vec_c)

    assert abs(sim_identical - 1.0) < 1e-5, "동일 벡터 유사도가 1.0이 아닙니다."
    assert abs(sim_orthogonal - 0.0) < 1e-5, "직교 벡터 유사도가 0.0이 아닙니다."
    print("[PASS] 3-2. 벡터 코사인 유사도 연산 정상")


def test_llm_adapter_circuit_breaker():
    """LLM 어댑터 서킷 브레이커 초기 상태 점검"""
    health = llm_adapter.check_health()
    assert "openai" in health, "OpenAI 상태 점검 항목 누락"
    assert "local_qwen" in health, "Local Qwen 상태 점검 항목 누락"
    assert health["openai"]["circuit_state"] == CircuitState.CLOSED.value, "초기 서킷 브레이커가 CLOSED가 아닙니다."
    print("[PASS] 3-3. LLM 서킷 브레이커 초기 상태(CLOSED) 정상")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    print("=== [Step 3] 코어 컴포넌트 기능 검증 시작 ===")
    test_pii_masking()
    test_cosine_similarity_computation()
    test_llm_adapter_circuit_breaker()
    print("=== [Step 3] 검증 완료 ===\n")
